# Adversarial Reviewer Mandate — Diff Mode

You are an isolated adversarial code reviewer. You have no relationship with
the author of this change, no stake in it shipping, and no memory of how it
was produced. Your one mandate: **find the problems the author did not.**

The user message contains a JSON review packet:
`diff` (the change), `acceptance_criteria` (what the change must satisfy),
`file_tree` (repository shape), `constraints` (project-level blocking rules),
`mode`, `phase`, `target`.

## What to hunt for

1. **Spec violations** — acceptance criteria the diff does not actually
   satisfy, satisfies only partially, or satisfies only on the happy path.
2. **Correctness** — logic errors, inverted conditions, off-by-one, broken
   error handling, race conditions, resource leaks, unhandled edge cases.
3. **Regressions** — behavior the diff removes or changes that existing code
   or the file tree suggests something else depends on.
4. **Security** — injection, path traversal, secret exposure, missing input
   validation at boundaries, unsafe deserialization, authz gaps.
5. **Constraint breaches** — anything in `constraints` the diff violates.
6. **Silent failure** — swallowed errors, defaults that mask failure, dead
   fallback paths.

## Rules

- Judge only what is in the packet. Do not assume unstated intent.
- Every finding must cite concrete evidence: a hunk, a line, a criterion ID,
  or a constraint sentence. No vibes.
- Do not pad. If something is genuinely fine, do not manufacture findings —
  but if you report **zero** findings you MUST include a non-empty top-level
  `checked_classes` array listing each failure class you checked and why it
  does not apply. A zero-finding report without it is rejected.
- No praise, no hedging, no summaries of what the change does well.
- Severity: `CRITICAL` = would ship a defect, spec violation, or constraint
  breach; `WARNING` = real risk or maintainability problem, not blocking;
  `SUGGESTION` = improvement, optional.

## Output — JSON only, no prose around it

```json
{
  "findings": [
    {
      "severity": "CRITICAL | WARNING | SUGGESTION",
      "file": "path/from/repo/root",
      "line": 0,
      "claim": "one-sentence statement of the defect",
      "evidence": "the hunk/criterion/constraint that proves it",
      "suggested_fix": "concrete fix (optional)"
    }
  ],
  "checked_classes": ["required non-empty ONLY when findings is empty: class checked — why it does not apply"]
}
```

`line` may be omitted when not applicable. Output nothing except this JSON
object.

Verified-context letter signing shared by every shell.\n+//! Identity and practice authority come only from [`ClinicalContext`].\n+\n+use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\n+use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n+use uuid::Uuid;\n+\n+use crate::{\n+    AppServices,\n+    affirmation::ClinicalContext,\n+    domain::{LetterId, LetterStatus},\n+    session::Principal,\n+};\n+\n+/// Transport input. The service checks it before the\n+/// write; the database checks the underlying rows again while locked.\n+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n+#[serde(rename_all = \"camelCase\")]\n+pub struct SigningTarget {\n+    pub letter_id: LetterId,\n+    pub case_id: Uuid,\n+    pub letter_version: i32,\n+    pub qa_revision: i64,\n+    pub signature_version: #[error(\"letter signing denied\")]\n+    Denied,\n+    #[error(\"letter not found\")]\n+    NotFound,\n+    #[error(\"letter or QA revision is stale\")]\n+    RevisionConflict,\n+    #[error(\"signature revision is stale\")]\n+    SignatureConflict,\n+    #[error(\"letter is not the current approved revision\")]\n+    NotApproved,\n+    #[error(\"case has not been affirmed at the surgeon gate\")]\n+    GateNotAffirmed,\n+    #[error(\"letter QA is incomplete or has a blocking failure\")]\n+    QaIncomplete,\n+    #[error(\"letter assertions do not have complete sources\")]\n+    SourceIncomplete,\n+    #[error(\"command identifier already has a different payload\")]\n+    CommandConflict,\n+    #[error(\"letter signing service unavailable\")]\n+    Unavailable,\n+    #[error(\"native authentication unavailable\")]\n+    NativeAuthenticationUnavailable,\n+}\n+\n+impl AppServices {\n+    fn check_signing_context(&self, context: &ClinicalContext) -> Result<(), SigningError> {\n+        if context.expires_at <= self.clock.now() {\n+            return Err(SigningError::Unauthenticated);\n+        }\n+        if context.principal != Principal::User {\n+            return Err(SigningError::Denied);\n+        }\n+        Ok(())\n+    }\n+\n+    fn check_signing_target(\n+        target: &SigningTarget,\n+        command: &SignLetterCommand,\n+    ) -> Result<(), SigningError> {\n+        if target.letter_id != command.letter_id\n+            || target.letter_version != command.expected_letter_version\n+            || target.qa_revision != command.expected_qa_revision\n+        {\n+            return Err(SigningError::RevisionConflict);\n+        }\n+        if target.signature_version != Some(command.expected_signature_version) {\n+            return Err(SigningError::SignatureConflict);\n+        }\n+        if target.status != LetterStatus::Approved\n+            || !target.approved_by_actor\n+            || !target.is_current\n+        {\n+            return Err(SigningError::NotApproved);\n+        }\n+        if !target.gate_affirmed {\n+            return Err(SigningError::GateNotAffirmed);\n+        }\n+        if !target.qa_complete {\n+            return Err(SigningError::QaIncomplete);\n+        }\n+        if !target.sources_complete {\n+            return Err(SigningError::SourceIncomplete);\n+        }\n+        Ok(())\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Checks fresh human context, resource authority and every signing\n+    /// precondition before invoking the repository's independent transaction.\n+    pub async fn execute_sign_letter(\n+        &self,\n+        context: &ClinicalContext,\n+        command: &SignLetterCommand,\n+    ) -> Result<SignLetterResult, SigningError> {\n+        self.check_signing_context(context)?;\n+        if let Some(original) = self\n+            .letters\n+            .lookup_sign_letter_command(context, command.command_id)\n+            .await?\n+        {\n+            if original.command_id != command.command_id\n+                || original.letter_id != command.letter_id.0\n+                || original.letter_version != command.expected_letter_version\n+                || original.qa_revision != command.expected_qa_revision\n+                || original.signature_version != command.expected_signature_version\n+            {\n+                return Err(SigningError::CommandConflict);\n+            }\n+            if !self\n+                .authority\n+                .may_sign_letter(context, LetterId(original.letter_id))\n+                .await?\n+            {\n+                return Err(SigningError::Denied);\n+            }\n+            return Ok(original);\n+        }\n+        if !self\n+            .authority\n+            .may_sign_letter(context, command.letter_id)\n+            .await?\n+        {\n+            return Err(SigningError::Denied);\n+        }\n+        let target = self\n+            .letters\n+            .read_signing_target(context, command.letter_id)\n+            .await?;\n+        Self::check_signing_target(&target, command)?;\n+        // Authority and session can change while repository reads wait.\n+        self.check_signing_context(context)?;\n+        if !self\n+            .authority\n+            .may_sign_letter(context, command.letter_id)\n+            .await?\n+        {\n+            return Err(SigningError::Denied);\n+        }\n+        self.letters.execute_sign_letter(context, command).await\n+    }\n+\n+    pub SigningError> {\n+        self.check_signing_context(context)?;\n+        if !self.authority.may_sign_letter(context, letter_id).await? {\n+            return Err(SigningError::Denied);\n+        }\n+        Ok(self\n+            .letters\n+            .lookup_sign_letter_command(context, command_id)\n+            .await?\n+            .filter(|result| result.letter_id == letter_id.0))\n+    }\n+\n+    pub async fn read_signing_target(\n+        &self,\n+        context: &ClinicalContext,\n+        letter_id: LetterId,\n+    ) -> Result<SigningTarget, SigningError> {\n+ );\n+    response\n+}\n+\n+fn error(status: StatusCode, code: &str) -> Response {\n+    (status, Json(json!({\"error\":code}))).into_response()\n+}\n+\n+fn invalid() -> Response {\n+ (StatusCode::SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, \"reassessment_unavailable\")\n+        }\n+    };\n+    error(status, code)\n+}\n+\n+fn context_error(value: ClinicalContextError) -> Response {\n+    match value Some(\"42501\") => GateError::Denied,\n+        Some(\"P0002\") => GateError::NotFound,\n+        Some(\"23505\") => GateError::CommandConflict,\n+        _ => GateError::Unavailable,\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+fn signing_failure(error: sqlx::Error) -> SigningError {\n+    match error.as_database_error().and_then(|e| e.code()).as_deref() {\n+        Some(\"42501\") => SigningError::Denied,\n+ {\n+    match error {\n+        GateError::Unauthenticated => SigningError::Unauthenticated,\n+        GateError::Denied => SigningError::Denied,\n+        GateError::NotFound => GateError::CommandConflict => ReassessmentError::CommandConflict,\n+        GateError::Unavailable | GateError::NativeAuthenticationUnavailable => {\n+            ReassessmentError::Unavailable\n+        }\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+impl PgGateRepository {\n+    pub async fn connect(url: &str) -> Result<Self, GateError> {\n+        let pool = PgPoolOptions::new()\n+            .max_connections(5)\n+            .acquire_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))\n+            .connect(url)\n+            .await\n+            .map_err(failure)?;\n+        let allowed: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(ROLE_CHECK)\n+            .fetch_one(&pool)\n+            .await\n+            .map_err(failure)?;\n+        if !allowed {\n+            return Err(GateError::Unavailable);\n+        }\n+        Ok(Self { pool })\n+    }\n+\n+    async fn begin(\n+        &self,\n+        context: &ClinicalContext,\n+    ) -> Result<Transaction<'static, Postgres>, GateError> {\n+        let mut tx = self.pool.begin().await.map_err(failure)?;\n+        let allowed: bool = sqlx::query_scalar(ROLE_CHECK)\n+            .fetch_one(&mut *tx)\n+            .await\n+            .map_err(failure)?;\n+        if !allowed {\n+            return Err(GateError::Unavailable);\n+        }\n+        sqlx::query(\"SET LOCAL ROLE aso_gate_executor\")\n+            .execute(&mut *tx)\n+            .await\n+            .map_err(failure)?;\n+        sqlx::query(\"SET LOCAL search_path = pg_catalog, aso, pg_temp\")\n+            .execute(&mut *tx)\n+            .await\n+            .map_err(failure)?;\n+ .bind(context.practice.to_string())\n+        .bind(principal)\n+        .bind(context.expires_at.to_rfc3339())\n+        .execute(&mut *tx)\n+        .await\n+        .map_err(failure)?;\n+        Ok(tx)\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+#[async_trait]\n+impl CaseRepository for PgGateRepository {\n+    async fn gate_state(&self, _: CaseId) -> Result<GateState, DomainError> {\n+ It keeps an unmounted legacy port honest until its\n+//! authoritative PostgreSQL implementation is scheduled.\n+\n+use aso_host::{\n+    domain::{CriterionId, DomainError},\n+    ports::{CriteriaRepository, Criterion},\n+};\n+use async_trait::async_trait;\n+\n+pub struct UnavailableCriteriaRepository;\n+\n+#[async_trait]\n+impl CriteriaRepository for UnavailableCriteriaRepository {\n+    async fn get(&self, _: CriterionId) -> Result<Criterion, DomainError> {\n+        Err(DomainError::Storage(\n+            \"authoritative criteria repository unavailable\".into(),\n+        ))\n+    }\n+\n+    async fn live_for_payer(&self, _: &str) -> Result<Vec<Criterion>, DomainError> {\n+        Err(DomainError::Storage(\n+            \"authoritative criteria repository unavailable\".into(),\n+        ))\n+    }\n+}\n\ndiff --git a/crates/aso-web-server/src/migrations.rs b/crates/aso-web-server/src/migrations.rs\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..d3da904\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/crates/aso-web-server/src/migrations.rs\n@@ -0,0 +1,73 @@\n+//! Explicit deployment operation. Ordinary server startup never reads migration\n+//! credentials or runs DDL. The existing ASO schema is a prerequisite.\n+\n+use sqlx::{\n+    migrate::{Migration, MigrationSource, MigrationType, Migrator},\n+    postgres::PgPoolOptions,\n+};\n+use std::{future::Future, pin::Pin};\n+\n+#[derive(Debug)]\n+struct ServerMigrations;\n+\n+impl MigrationSource<'static> for ServerMigrations {\n+    fn resolve(\n+        self,\n+    ) -> Pin<Box<dyn Future<Output = Result<Vec<Migration>, sqlx::error::BoxDynError>> + Send>>\n+    {\n+        Box::pin(async {\n+            Ok(vec![\n+                Migration::new(\n+                    2026090601,\n+                    \"durable gate\".into(),\n+                    MigrationType::Simple,\n+                    include_str!(\"../../../migrations/server/2026090601_durable_gate.sql\").into(),\n+                    false,\n+                ),\n+                Migration::new(\n+                    2026090602,\n+                    \"durable signing\".into(),\n+                    MigrationType::Simple,\n+                    include_str!(\"../../../migrations/server/2026090602_durable_signing.sql\")\n+                        .into(),\n+                    false,\n+                ),\n+                Migration::new(\n+                    2026090603,\n+                    \"durable evidence reassessment\".into(),\n+                    MigrationType::Simple,\n+                    include_str!(\"../../../migrations/server/2026090603_durable_reassessment.sql\")\n+                        .into(),\n+                    false,\n+                ),\n+            ])\n+        })\n+    }\n+}\n+\n+pub async fn run() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n+    let url = std::env::var(\"ASO_MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL\")\n+        .map_err(|_| \"ASO_MIGRATION_DATABASE_URL is required for --migrate-server\")?;\n+    let pool = PgPoolOptions::new()\n+        .max_connections(1)\n+        .connect(&url)\n+        .await\n+        .map_err(|_| \"server migration connection failed\")?;\n+    // SQLx takes its migration lock, validates SHA-384 checksums, and commits\n+    // each migration and its ledger entry in one transaction.\n+    let result = Migrator::new(ServerMigrations).await?.run(&pool).await;\n+    pool.close().await;\n+    match result {\n+        Ok(()) => {\n+            println!(\"server migrations applied and checksums verified\");\n+            Ok(())\n+        }\n+        Err(sqlx::migrate::MigrateError::VersionMismatch(_)) => {\n+            Err(\"server migration checksum mismatch: applied SQL was modified\".into())\n+        }\n+        // SQL errors can contain record values. Keep deployment output generic.\n+        Err(_) => {\n+            Err(\"server migration failed; database unchanged for the failed migration\".into())\n+        }\n+    }\n+}\n\ndiff --git a/migrations/server/2026090602_durable_signing.sql b/migrations/server/2026090602_durable_signing.sql\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..c3e0df8\n--- /dev/null\n+++ b/migrations/server/2026090602_durable_signing.sql\n@@ -0,0 +1,489 @@\n+-- RA-03. Verified-context signing over a dedicated clinical command ledger.\n+-- Lane: server-authoritative relational. Privacy: local. Neither signing\n+-- commands nor their immutable results are eligible for client replay.\n+\n+ALTER TABLE aso.letters\n+  ADD COLUMN qa_revision bigint NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n+  ADD COLUMN approved_qa_revision bigint;\n+\n+-- Existing approved rows retain their exact attached QA generation. Disable\n+-- only the legacy signing/touch triggers while the new columns are backfilled.\n+ALTER TABLE aso.letters DISABLE TRIGGER letters_signing_rules;\n+ALTER TABLE aso.letters DISABLE TRIGGER letters_touch;\n+WITH qa AS (\n+  SELECT l.id, count(r.id)::bigint AS count\n+  FROM aso.letters l\n+  LEFT JOIN aso.letter_qa_results r ON r.letter_id = l.id\n+  GROUP BY l.id\n+)\n+UPDATE aso.letters l\n+SET qa_revision = qa.count,\n+    approved_qa_revision = CASE\n+      WHEN l.status IN ('approved', 'signed') THEN qa.count\n+      ELSE NULL\n+    END\n+FROM qa\n+WHERE qa.id = l.id;\n+ALTER TABLE aso.letters ENABLE TRIGGER letters_touch;\n+ALTER TABLE aso.letters ENABLE TRIGGER letters_signing_rules;\n+\n+CREATE TABLE aso.letter_sign_commands (\n+  kratos_identity_id uuid NOT NULL,\n+  practice_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES aso.practices(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,\n+  command_id uuid NOT NULL,\n+  actor_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES aso.users(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,\n+  case_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES aso.cases(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,\n+  letter_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES aso.letters(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,\n+  expected_letter_version integer NOT NULL CHECK (expected_letter_version > 0),\n+  expected_qa_revision bigint NOT NULL CHECK (expected_qa_revision >= 0),\n+  expected_signature_version integer NOT NULL CHECK (expected_signature_version > 0),\n+  signature_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES aso.signatures(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,\n+  result jsonb NOT NULL,\n+  committed_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\n+  PRIMARY KEY (kratos_identity_id, practice_id, command_id),\n+  UNIQUE (letter_id)\n+);\n+COMMENT ON TABLE aso.letter_sign_commands IS\n+  'Lane: server-authoritative relational. Privacy: local. Both operations use the durable command ledger, refuse caller-selected clinical identity, repeat authority and revision checks in `AppServices` and PostgreSQL, and retain the three evidence states\u2014`met`, `gap`, and `void`\u2014as separate committed values.\n\n## Delivered behavior\n\nThe mounted `aso-web-server` composes `api_router(state)` with restricted PostgreSQL clinical repositories. Signing and evidence routes resolve a fresh verified session and call `AppServices`; their request bodies contain command and clinical data, with no authority-bearing actor field. Gate policy performs its own read-only capability and resource check before the service and database checks run.\n\nSigning requires the current letter, QA, and signature revisions, a committed gate, completed QA, and cited source assertions. Reassessment requires the current evidence revision and clinical authority. Each operation checks the immutable scoped command result before mutable target reads, so an exact repeat or explicit lookup resolves a response lost after commit without creating another clinical effect.\n\nThe live PostgreSQL receipts remain bound to current source hashes. Their selected markers cover forged and stale signing refusal, independent administrator/agent/foreign-scope refusal, lost-response reconciliation, `void` to `gap`, and complete `met`/`gap`/`void` transitions with one audit and one command receipt per transition. Every disposable database and role cleanup entry passed.\n\nSigning, affirmation, and reassessment are absent from the shared sync/PEM replay path. The React feature hooks send these commands through feature APIs and the shared HTTP client.\n\n## Caller truth\n\nThe HTTP behavior is mounted in production composition. The evidence timeline route mounts the read component, and its hook contains reassessment and lookup operations, but the component exposes no reassessment control. The surgeon-gate route is still a placeholder, so its hook is not a production browser caller. There is no browser signing caller.\n\nDesktop exposes typed wrappers that fail closed with `NativeAuthenticationUnavailable`. The desktop crate has no Tauri dependency, `#[tauri::command]`, or invoke-handler registration. RA-03 therefore establishes matching operation contracts and refusal behavior for the inactive desktop boundary; it does not establish runnable Tauri parity.\n\nThis is the uncomfortable delivery limit: server-side clinical parity is real, while an end user cannot yet invoke the new clinical mutations from either the mounted browser interface or a Tauri window. Later runtime changes own those surfaces.\n\n## Observed verification\n\nRust Tier 0 used the installed `1.97.1` toolchain because the project-stated `1.94` toolchain is unavailable. `cargo check` and `cargo clippy --no-deps` exited zero for `aso-host`, `aso-server-axum`, `aso-web-server`, and `aso-desktop`.\n\nFocused Rust Tier 1 results:\n\n- `aso-host` signing: 5 passed; reassessment: 4 passed.\n- `aso-server-axum` letters: 5 passed; evidence: 4 passed; gate: 11 passed.\n- `aso-web-server` deployment composition: 2 passed.\n- `aso-desktop` unavailable-boundary contracts: 4 passed.\n\n`pnpm --dir web typecheck` and `pnpm --dir web lint` exited zero. An earlier command included an extra argument separator and ran the broad suite unintentionally; 143 tests passed and one unrelated graph-session-manager test timed out. That timeout is recorded as an out-of-scope observed failure and is not represented as a passing broad-suite result.\n\nPython AST parsing passed for seven affected verification scripts.
